    primitive / primitives

    Lucide Icon

    Lucide Icon primitive

    Install

    npx ui8kit add lucide-icon

    Pulls in: utils

    Preview

    Code

    Point the CLI at this runtime once, then add the item:

    npx ui8kit init --yes --framework svelte --dir src --registry-url https://registry.buildy.tw/svelte --strict-cdn
    npx ui8kit add lucide-icon
    primitives/lucide-icon/lucide-icon.svelte

    Svelte registry item (JSON)

    Point the CLI at this runtime once, then add the item:

    npx ui8kit init --yes --framework react --dir src --registry-url https://registry.buildy.tw/react --strict-cdn
    npx ui8kit add lucide-icon
    primitives/lucide-icon/lucide-icon.tsx
    // Code generated by xgen. DO NOT EDIT.
// Source: xgen/specs/ui/lucide-icon.json
/**
 * LucideIcon: a Lucide glyph (ISC license) by kebab-case name, drawn from the registry next to this file.
 * Decorative (aria-hidden) unless a `label` is given.
 */
import { createElement } from "react";
import { cn } from "../../utils";
import { LUCIDE } from "./lucide-icons";

export type LucideIconProps = { name: string; size?: number; strokeWidth?: number; label?: string; className?: string };

export function LucideIcon({ name, size = 16, strokeWidth = 2, label, className }: LucideIconProps) {
  const nodes = LUCIDE[name] ?? LUCIDE["circle"] ?? [];
  return (
    <svg
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      width={size}
      height={size}
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      className={cn("inline-block shrink-0", className)}
      aria-hidden={label ? undefined : true}
      aria-label={label}
      role={label ? "img" : undefined}
      data-slot="icon"
      data-lucide={name}
    >
      {nodes.map(([tag, attrs], i) => createElement(tag, { key: i, ...attrs }))}

    Vue registry item (JSON)

    Props

    Names and types come from the xgen specs of the source. Standard HTML attributes (id, role, tabIndex, aria-*) pass through to the root element; the class prop is className in React.

    LucideIcon renders an svg glyph by name.

    Props of LucideIcon
    PropTypeDefaultDescription
    name string-kebab-case Lucide name; an unknown name draws a circle
    size int-width and height in px, 16 by default
    strokeWidth int-stroke width, 2 by default
    label string-accessible name; without it the icon is decorative
    class string-adds Tailwind utilities
